ts-node\nimport { basename, dirname, join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nimport ts from 'typescript';\n\nimport {\n  collectFiles,\n  filterFilesBySnapshot,\n  isArchivedWorkspacePath,\n  normalizeWorkspacePath,\n  readWorkspaceFile,\n  requireScanRoots,\n  resolveInsideWorkspace as resolveAdapterPath,\n  workspacePathExists,\n} from './adapter-fs';\n\ntype FindingRule =\n  | 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test'\n  | 'migration_without_test'\n  | 'security_source_without_security_test';\n\ninterface AdapterInput {\n  readonly roots?: readonly string[];\n  readonly allowlist?: readonly string[];\n  readonly includeWriteBoundaryFindings?: boolean;\n  readonly repo_snapshot?: { readonly allowed_paths?: readonly string[]; readonly snapshot_hash?: string; readonly repo_state_id?: string };\n}\n\ninterface EvidenceRef {\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterObservation {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly type: string;\n  readonly path?: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly name?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterFinding {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly rule: FindingRule;\n  readonly severity: 'medium' | 'high';\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly message: string;\n  readonly evidence: readonly EvidenceRef[];\n  readonly confidence?: number;\n  readonly actionability?: 'actionable' | 'review_required';\n  readonly review_reason?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface MemoryCandidate {\n  readonly belief_id: string;\n  readonly claim: string;\n  readonly confidence: number;\n  readonly evidence_refs: readonly string[];\n  readonly source_tool_id: string;\n}\n\ninterface AriaOutput {\n  readonly observations: readonly AdapterObservation[];\n  readonly findings: readonly AdapterFinding[];\n  readonly read_paths: readonly string[];\n  readonly evidence_sources: readonly string[];\n  readonly belief_candidates: readonly MemoryCandidate[];\n  readonly cost_units: number;\n  readonly metadata: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface FileUnit {\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly relativePath: string;\n  readonly text: string;\n  readonly sourceFile: ts.SourceFile;\n  readonly isTest: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface SourceRisk {\n  readonly riskClass: string;\n  readonly highRisk: boolean;\n  readonly securitySensitive: boolean;\n  readonly migrationHazard: boolean;\n  readonly writeBoundary: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface AnalysisResult {\n  readonly observations: AdapterObservation[];\n  readonly findings: AdapterFinding[];\n  readonly readPaths: string[];\n}\n\ninterface PathAlias {\n  readonly prefix: string;\n  readonly suffix: string;\n  readonly targets: readonly string[];\n}\n\n\nexport function analyzeTestGaps(input: AdapterInput, workspaceRoot = process.cwd()): AriaOutput {\n  const roots = requireScanRoots('test-gap-adapter', input.roots);\n  const allowlist = new Set((input.allowlist ?? []).map(normalizePath));\n  const files = roots\n    .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n    .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n    .flatMap((root) => collectSourceAndTestFiles(root));\n  const snapshotFiles = filterFilesBySnapshot(files, workspaceRoot, input);\n  const units = snapshotFiles.map((file) => readFileUnit(file, workspaceRoot));\n  const tests = units.filter((unit) => unit.isTest);\n  const sources = units.filter((unit) => !unit.isTest);\n  const sourcePaths = new Set(sources.map((source) => source.relativePath));\n  const pathAliases = readPathAliases(workspaceRoot);\n  const result: AnalysisResult = {\n    observations: [],\n    findings: [],\n    readPaths: units.map((unit) => unit.relativePath),\n  };\n\n  for (const test of tests) {\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-test-file:${test.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_test_file',\n      path: test.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(test.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        imports: importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).sort(),\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  let highRiskWithoutTest = 0;\n  let directMatchCount = 0;\n  let weakMatchCount = 0;\n  for (const source of sources) {\n    const risk = classifySourceRisk(source);\n    if (!risk.highRisk && !risk.securitySensitive && !risk.migrationHazard) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    const matchedTests = matchingTests(source, tests, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n    const weakMatchedTests = weakSymbolTests(source, tests).filter(\n      (test) => !matchedTests.some((matched) => matched.relativePath === test.relativePath),\n    );\n    directMatchCount += matchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n    weakMatchCount += weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n    const allowlisted = allowlist.has(source.relativePath);\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-source-file:${source.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_source_file',\n      path: source.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(source.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        riskClass: risk.riskClass,\n        highRisk: risk.highRisk,\n        securitySensitive: risk.securitySensitive,\n        migrationHazard: risk.migrationHazard,\n        writeBoundary: risk.writeBoundary,\n        matchedTests: matchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n        weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n        allowlisted,\n      },\n    });\n    if (allowlisted || matchedTests.length > 0) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (risk.migrationHazard) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `migration-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'migration_without_test',\n        severity: 'high',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'Hazardous migration has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.55 : 0.88,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (risk.securitySensitive) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `security-source-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'security_source_without_security_test',\n        severity: 'high',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'Security-sensitive source file has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.55 : 0.86,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (input.includeWriteBoundaryFindings === true && risk.highRisk && risk.writeBoundary) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `high-risk-source-without-adjacent-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test',\n        severity: 'medium',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'High-risk source file has no adjacent, sibling __tests__, or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.5 : 0.75,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  result.observations.push({\n    id: 'test-gap-coverage-summary',\n    type: 'test_gap_coverage_summary',\n    details: {\n      sourceFiles: sources.length,\n      testFiles: tests.length,\n      directMatchCount,\n      weakMatchCount,\n      highRiskWithoutTest,\n      unmatchedHighRiskCount: highRiskWithoutTest,\n      scanMode: 'import_graph_v1',\n    },\n  });\n\n  result.observations.sort(compareById);\n  result.findings.sort(compareById);\n  result.readPaths.sort();\n  const evidenceSources = Array.from(\n    new Set(result.findings.flatMap((finding) => finding.evidence.map((evidence) => evidence.path))),\n  ).sort();\n\n  return {\n    observations: result.observations,\n    findings: result.findings,\n    read_paths: Array.from(new Set(result.readPaths)).sort(),\n    evidence_sources: evidenceSources,\n    belief_candidates: [\n      {\n        belief_id: 'test-gap:high-risk-source-coverage-surface',\n        claim: 'high-risk backend, web security, and migration surfaces can be mapped to adjacent or importing test coverage signals',\n        confidence: 0.75,\n        evidence_refs: ['apps/**/*.ts', 'libs/**/*.ts', 'platform/libs/**/*.ts', 'web/**/*.tsx'],\n        source_tool_id: 'test-gap-adapter',\n      },\n    ],\n    cost_units: Array.from(new Set(result.readPaths)).length,\n    metadata: {\n      adapter: 'test-gap-adapter',\n      roots: roots.map(String).sort(),\n      files_scanned: snapshotFiles.length,\n      findings_count: result.findings.length,\n      allowlist_count: allowlist.size,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction classifySourceRisk(unit: FileUnit): SourceRisk {\n  const path = unit.relativePath;\n  const text = unit.text;\n  const riskClass = riskClassForPath(path);\n  const highRisk = /(\\.controller|\\.resolver|\\.handler|\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\.middleware|\\.interceptor)\\.ts$/.test(path);\n  const securitySensitive = /dangerouslySetInnerHTML|DOMPurify|sanitizeHtml|@Public\\b|AuthGuard|TenantGuard|PermissionGuard/i.test(text)\n    || /(\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\/auth\\/|\\/security\\/|\\/permission)/i.test(path);\n  const writeBoundary = /@(Post|Put|Patch|Delete|Mutation)\\b|\\b(create|update|delete|remove|archive|approve|reject|reset|invite)\\w*\\s*\\(/i.test(text);\n  const migrationHazard = /\\/migrations\\/.*\\.ts$/.test(path)\n    && /\\b(DROP|DELETE|TRUNCATE|ALTER\\s+TYPE|ENABLE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY|FORCE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY)\\b/i.test(text);\n  return { riskClass, highRisk, securitySensitive, migrationHazard, writeBoundary };\n}\n\nfunction riskClassForPath(path: string): string {\n  if (path.includes('/migrations/')) {\n    return 'migration';\n  }\n  // match[1] is narrowed explicitly so the file stays clean under\n  // --noUncheckedIndexedAccess (the capture group is structurally guaranteed,\n  // but the index signature cannot prove it to the compiler).\n  const match = path.match(/\\.([a-z-]+)\\.(ts|tsx)$/);\n  if (match?.[1] !== undefined) {\n    return match[1];\n  }\n  if (path.endsWith('.tsx')) {\n    return 'ui_component';\n  }\n  return 'source';\n}\n\nfunction matchingTests(\n  source: FileUnit,\n  tests: readonly FileUnit[],\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const sourceBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(source.relativePath);\n  const sourceDir = dirname(source.relativePath);\n  return tests.filter((test) => {\n    const testBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(test.relativePath);\n    if (testBase === sourceBase) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath) === sourceDir && testBase.startsWith(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath).endsWith(`${sourceDir}/__tests__`) && testBase.includes(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    return importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).some((specifier) => {\n      const resolved = resolveImportSpecifier(test.relativePath, specifier, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n      return resolved === source.relativePath;\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction weakSymbolTests(source: FileUnit, tests: readonly FileUnit[]): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const symbolNames = exportedSymbols(source.sourceFile);\n  if (symbolNames.length === 0) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  return tests.filter((test) => symbolNames.some((symbolName) => symbolName.length > 2 && test.text.includes(symbolName)));\n}\n\nfunction basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(path: string): string {\n  return basename(path)\n    .replace(/\\.(spec|test)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(controller|resolver|handler|guard|strategy|middleware|interceptor|service|entity|module|component)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '');\n}\n\nfunction importedSpecifiers(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const imports: string[] = [];\n  for (const statement of sourceFile.statements) {\n    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(statement) && ts.isStringLiteral(statement.moduleSpecifier)) {\n      imports.push(statement.moduleSpecifier.text);\n    }\n  }\n  return imports;\n}\n\nfunction resolveImportSpecifier(\n  importingPath: string,\n  specifier: string,\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): string | undefined {\n  const candidates: string[] = [];\n  if (specifier.startsWith('.')) {\n    candidates.push(normalizePath(join(dirname(importingPath), specifier)));\n  } else {\n    for (const alias of pathAliases) {\n      if (!specifier.startsWith(alias.prefix) || !specifier.endsWith(alias.suffix)) {\n        continue;\n      }\n      const middle = specifier.slice(alias.prefix.length, specifier.length - alias.suffix.length);\n      for (const target of alias.targets) {\n        candidates.push(target.replace('*', middle));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  for (const candidate of candidates.flatMap(candidatePathVariants)) {\n    if (sourcePaths.has(candidate)) {\n      return candidate;\n    }\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction candidatePathVariants(candidate: string): string[] {\n  const normalized = normalizePath(candidate).replace(/^\\.\\//, '');\n  return [\n    normalized,\n    `${normalized}.ts`,\n    `${normalized}.tsx`,\n    `${normalized}/index.ts`,\n    `${normalized}/index.tsx`,\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction readPathAliases(workspaceRoot: string): readonly PathAlias[] {\n  const path = resolve(workspaceRoot, 'tsconfig.base.json');\n  if (!workspacePathExists(path)) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  try {\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(readWorkspaceFile(path)) as {\n      compilerOptions?: { paths?: Record<string, readonly string[]> };\n    };\n    return Object.entries(parsed.compilerOptions?.paths ?? {}).map(([key, targets]) => {\n      const starIndex = key.indexOf('*');\n      return {\n        prefix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(0, starIndex) : key,\n        suffix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(starIndex + 1) : '',\n        targets: targets.map((target) => normalizePath(target)),\n      };\n    });\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\nfunction exportedSymbols(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const symbols = new Set<string>();\n  visit(sourceFile, (node) => {\n    if ((ts.isClassDeclaration(node) || ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) || ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(node) || ts.isTypeAliasDeclaration(node)) && node.name) {\n      const modifiers = ts.canHaveModifiers(node) ? ts.getModifiers(node) ?? [] : [];\n      if (modifiers.some((modifier) => modifier.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ExportKeyword)) {\n        symbols.add(node.name.text);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n  return Array.from(symbols).sort();\n}\n\nfunction readFileUnit(path: string, workspaceRoot: string): FileUnit {\n  const text = readWorkspaceFile(path);\n  return {\n    path,\n    relativePath: normalizePath(relative(workspaceRoot, path)),\n    text,\n    sourceFile: ts.createSourceFile(path, text, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true),\n    isTest: isTestFile(path),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction collectSourceAndTestFiles(root: string): readonly string[] {\n  return collectFiles(root, {\n    extensions: ['.ts', '.tsx'],\n    // E13 FP class (2): archived corpus (e.g. migrations/.archive/<timestamp>/)\n    // is dead code — it can neither need test coverage nor PROVIDE it (an\n    // archived spec must not satisfy a live source), so it is excluded from\n    // the scan entirely rather than special-cased at finding time.\n    includeFile: (name, path) => !name.endsWith('.d.ts') && !isArchivedWorkspacePath(path),\n  });\n}\n\nfunction isTestFile(path: string): boolean {\n  const normalized = normalizePath(path);\n  return /(__tests__|\\.spec\\.|\\.test\\.)/.test(normalized);\n}\n\nfunction resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot: string, requestedPath: string): string {\n  return resolveAdapterPath(workspaceRoot, requestedPath);\n}\n\nfunction visit(node: ts.Node, visitor: (node: ts.Node) => void): void {\n  visitor(node);\n  ts.forEachChild(node, (child) => visit(child, visitor));\n}\n\nfunction compareById(a: { readonly id: string }, b: { readonly id: string }): number {\n  return a.id.localeCompare(b.id);\n}\n\nfunction normalizePath(path: string): string {\n  return normalizeWorkspacePath(path);\n}\n\nfunction readStdin(): Promise<string> {\n  return new Promise((resolvePromise, reject) => {\n    let input = '';\n    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');\n    // setEncoding('utf8') makes every chunk a string at runtime, but the\n    // stream's declared chunk type stays `string | Buffer` — concatenating the\n    // union is what the type checker rejects. Narrow at the boundary rather\n    // than widening `input` (kernel-dead-wire-adapter is the converged shape).\n    process.stdin.on('data', (chunk: string | Buffer) => {\n      input += typeof chunk === 'string' ? chunk : chunk.toString('utf8');\n    });\n    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolvePromise(input));\n    process.stdin.on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  const rawInput = await readStdin();\n  const input = rawInput.trim().length > 0 ? (JSON.parse(rawInput) as AdapterInput) : {};\n  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(analyzeTestGaps(input))}\\n`);\n}\n\nif (require.main === module) {\n  main().catch((error: unknown) => {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    process.stderr.write(`${message}\\n`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n}\n","numLines":494,"startLine":1,"totalLines":494}}}

* GraphQL Authentication Guard\n *\n * Validates JWT tokens for GraphQL requests in hr-service.\n * Provides defense-in-depth authentication at the resolver level.\n *\n * @module Common/Guards\n */\nimport {\n  Injectable,\n  Inject,\n  CanActivate,\n  ExecutionContext,\n  UnauthorizedException,\n  Logger,\n  SetMetadata,\n} from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\nimport { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';\nimport { GqlExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/graphql';\nimport { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';\nimport { Request } from 'express';\nimport { getJwtVerifyOptions, enforceAccessTokenType } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/auth';\n\n/**\n * Metadata key for public routes\n */\nexport const IS_PUBLIC_KEY = 'isPublic';\n\n/**\n * Public decorator - marks a route as publicly accessible\n */\nexport const Public = (): ReturnType<typeof SetMetadata> =>\n  SetMetadata(IS_PUBLIC_KEY, true);\n\n/**\n * JWT Payload interface\n */\nexport interface JwtPayload {\n  sub: string;\n  email: string;\n  tenantId: string;\n  roles: string[];\n  permissions?: string[];\n  iat: number;\n  exp: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Authenticated request with user info\n */\ninterface AuthenticatedRequest extends Request {\n  user?: JwtPayload;\n  userId?: string;\n  tenantId?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * GraphQL context with request\n */\ninterface GqlContext {\n  req?: AuthenticatedRequest;\n}\n\n/**\n * GqlAuthGuard\n * Validates JWT tokens for GraphQL requests\n * Supports public routes via @Public() decorator\n */\n@Injectable()\nexport class GqlAuthGuard implements CanActivate {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(GqlAuthGuard.name);\n\n  // WHY: Explicit @Inject() — design:paramtypes may not survive all build/runtime environments.\n  constructor(\n    @Inject(JwtService) private readonly jwtService: JwtService,\n    @Inject(ConfigService) private readonly configService: ConfigService,\n    @Inject(Reflector) private readonly reflector: Reflector,\n  ) {}\n\n  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {\n    // Check if route is marked as public\n    const isPublic = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<boolean>(IS_PUBLIC_KEY, [\n      context.getHandler(),\n      context.getClass(),\n    ]);\n\n    if (isPublic) {\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    // Get GraphQL context\n    const ctx = GqlExecutionContext.create(context);\n    const gqlContext = ctx.getContext<GqlContext>();\n    const request = gqlContext.req;\n\n    if (!request) {\n      // REST endpoint fallback\n      const httpContext = context.switchToHttp();\n      const httpRequest = httpContext.getRequest<AuthenticatedRequest>();\n      return this.validateRequest(httpRequest);\n    }\n\n    return this.validateRequest(request);\n  }\n\n  private async validateRequest(\n    request: AuthenticatedRequest,\n  ): Promise<boolean> {\n    // SECURITY (HIGH-04): request.user is set by UserContextMiddleware which parses the\n    // x-user-payload header injected by the gateway. This is trusted only because the\n    // gateway is assumed to be the sole entry point (network-level isolation).\n    // Defence-in-depth: require that a pre-set user also carries a non-empty roles array,\n    // which is always present in a genuine gateway-injected JWT payload but absent if an\n    // attacker crafts a minimal x-user-payload header manually.\n    if (\n      request.user &&\n      request.user.sub &&\n      request.user.tenantId &&\n      Array.isArray((request.user as JwtPayload).roles) &&\n      (request.user as JwtPayload).roles.length > 0\n    ) {\n      this.logger.debug(\n        `User ${request.user.sub} pre-authenticated for tenant ${request.user.tenantId}`,\n      );\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    const token = this.extractToken(request);\n\n    if (!token) {\n      throw new UnauthorizedException('Missing authentication token');\n    }\n\n    try {\n      // Use centralised JWT verification options from backend-common.\n      // BEFORE: verifyAsync() called with only { secret } — no algorithm restriction.\n      // Algorithm confusion attacks (RS256 downgrade) were possible.\n      // Tokens without iss/aud claims were silently accepted (no conditional checks here).\n      // AFTER: getJwtVerifyOptions() enforces all three at jsonwebtoken library level.\n      const payload = await this.jwtService.verifyAsync<JwtPayload>(\n        token,\n        getJwtVerifyOptions(this.configService),\n      );\n\n      // Defense-in-depth: subgraph guard also enforces token type.\n      const isProduction = this.configService.get<string>('NODE_ENV') === 'production';\n      enforceAccessTokenType(payload, this.logger, isProduction);\n\n      // Attach user info to request\n      request.user = payload;\n      request.userId = payload.sub;\n      request.tenantId = payload.tenantId;\n\n      this.logger.debug(\n        `User ${payload.sub} authenticated for tenant ${payload.tenantId}`,\n      );\n\n      return true;\n    } catch (error) {\n      this.logger.warn(`JWT validation failed: ${(error as Error).message}`);\n      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid or expired token');\n    }\n  }\n\n  private extractToken(request: AuthenticatedRequest): string | null {\n    const authHeader = request.headers?.authorization;\n\n    if (!authHeader) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    const parts = authHeader.split(' ');\n    const type = parts[0];\n    const token = parts[1];\n\n    if (type !== 'Bearer' || !token) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return token;\n  }\n}\n","numLines":184,"startLine":1,"totalLines":184}}}

'';\n85\t    const declStart = src.lastIndexOf('\\n', match.index ?? 0) + 1;\n86\t    const preamble = src.slice(declStart, (match.index ?? 0) + whole.length);\n87\t    if (/\\babstract\\s+class\\b/.test(preamble)) continue;\n88\t    if (!/\\bimplements\\b[^{]*\\bCanActivate\\b/.test(heritage)) continue;\n89\t    const ext = /\\bextends\\s+([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)/.exec(heritage);\n90\t    found.push({ className, file, extendsClass: ext?.[1] ?? null });\n91\t  }\n92\t  return found;\n93\t}\n94\t\n95\tconst IDENT = /[A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*/g;\n96\t\n97\t/** Identifiers inside one balanced `(` … `)` group starting at `open`. */\n98\tfunction balancedGroup(src: string, open: number, openCh: string, closeCh: string): string {\n99\t  let depth = 0;\n100\t  for (let i = open; i < src.length; i++) {\n101\t    if (src[i] === openCh) depth++;\n102\t    else if (src[i] === closeCh) {\n103\t      depth--;\n104\t      if (depth === 0) return src.slice(open + 1, i);\n105\t    }\n106\t  }\n107\t  return src.slice(open + 1);\n108\t}\n109\t\n110\t/** The object literal enclosing `at`: walk back to its `{`, forward to the matching `}`. */\n111\tfunction enclosingObject(src: string, at: number): string {\n112\t  let depth = 0;\n113\t  let start = -1;\n114\t  for (let i = at; i >= 0; i--) {\n115\t    if (src[i] === '}') depth++;\n116\t    else if (src[i] === '{') {\n117\t      if (depth === 0) {\n118\t        start = i;\n119\t        break;\n120\t      }\n121\t      depth--;\n122\t    }\n123\t  }\n124\t  if (start === -1) return '';\n125\t  return balancedGroup(src, start, '{', '}');\n126\t}\n127\t\n128\t/** Every class identifier a file wires as a guard, by any registration form. */\n129\tfunction registrationSites(src: string): Set<string> {\n130\t  const names = new Set<string>();\n131\t  const add = (text: string): void => {\n132\t    for (const id of text.match(IDENT) ?? * INVARIANT — no dead guards (ADR-0010, SEC-HIGH-060).\n *\n * `IpWhitelistGuard` sat in gateway-api for two years registered in no\n * module, under no `APP_GUARD`, in no `@UseGuards()`. It read as an access\n * control and controlled nothing. A guard class that exists but is wired to\n * no request path is worse than no guard: reviewers, auditors and the next\n * engineer all read it as protection.\n *\n * Rule: every non-abstract class implementing `CanActivate` under `apps/**`\n * and `libs/**` (production source, not tests) must be reachable from a\n * registration site:\n *   - an `APP_GUARD` provider (`useClass`, `useExisting`, or a `useFactory`\n *     that constructs it),\n *   - a `@UseGuards(...)` / `UseGuards(...)` call (controller, resolver,\n *     handler, or an `applyDecorators` bundle),\n *   - a `globalGuards: [...]` / `useGlobalGuards(...)` bootstrap list,\n *   - or a registered guard that `extends` it,\n * or carry an entry in `.claude/allowlists/unregistered-guards.yaml` with\n * `{owner, expiry, findingId, reason}`. Expired entries fail; entries whose\n * class no longer exists fail (the list only shrinks).\n *\n * The second block keeps both retired IP access-rule stacks deleted.\n */\n\nimport { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nimport * as yaml from 'js-yaml';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\nconst ALLOWLIST = resolve(REPO_ROOT, '.claude/allowlists/unregistered-guards.yaml');\n\n/** Production source only: tests, fixtures and built output are not registration evidence. */\nconst NOT_PRODUCTION =\n  /(^|\\/)(__tests__|__mocks__|test|tests|e2e|dist|\\.archive)\\/|\\.(spec|test)\\.tsx?$/;\n\nfunction listSourceFiles(): string[] {\n  return execFileSync(\n    'git',\n    [\n      '-C',\n      REPO_ROOT,\n      'ls-files',\n      '--cached',\n      '--others',\n      '--exclude-standard',\n      '--',\n      'apps',\n      'libs',\n      'platform',\n    ],\n    { encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 },\n  )\n    .split('\\n')\n    .filter((path) => path.endsWith('.ts') && !NOT_PRODUCTION.test(path));\n}\n\n/** Drop block + line comments so docstrings do not register as code. */\nfunction stripComments(src: string): string {\n  return src\n    .replace(/\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\//g, '')\n    .split('\\n')\n    .map((line) => line.replace(/(?<![:'\"`])\\/\\/.*$/, ''))\n    .join('\\n');\n}\n\ninterface GuardDefinition {\n  className: string;\n  file: string;\n  /** Direct superclass identifier, when the declaration has one. */\n  extendsClass: string | null;\n}\n\nconst CLASS_DECL = /\\bclass\\s+([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\s*([^{]*)\\{/g;\n\n/** Non-abstract classes whose declaration lists `CanActivate` among its interfaces. */\nfunction guardDefinitions(file: string, src: string): GuardDefinition[] {\n  const found: GuardDefinition[] = [];\n  for (const match of src.matchAll(CLASS_DECL)) {\n    const whole = match[0];\n    const className = match[1] ?? '';\n    const heritage = match[2] ?? '';\n    const declStart = src.lastIndexOf('\\n', match.index ?? 0) + 1;\n    const preamble = src.slice(declStart, (match.index ?? 0) + whole.length);\n    if (/\\babstract\\s+class\\b/.test(preamble)) continue;\n    if (!/\\bimplements\\b[^{]*\\bCanActivate\\b/.test(heritage)) continue;\n    const ext = /\\bextends\\s+([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)/.exec(heritage);\n    found.push({ className, file, extendsClass: ext?.[1] ?? null });\n  }\n  return found;\n}\n\nconst IDENT = /[A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*/g;\n\n/** Identifiers inside one balanced `(` … `)` group starting at `open`. */\nfunction balancedGroup(src: string, open: number, openCh: string, closeCh: string): string {\n  let depth = 0;\n  for (let i = open; i < src.length; i++) {\n    if (src[i] === openCh) depth++;\n    else if (src[i] === closeCh) {\n      depth--;\n      if (depth === 0) return src.slice(open + 1, i);\n    }\n  }\n  return src.slice(open + 1);\n}\n\n/** The object literal enclosing `at`: walk back to its `{`, forward to the matching `}`. */\nfunction enclosingObject(src: string, at: number): string {\n  let depth = 0;\n  let start = -1;\n  for (let i = at; i >= 0; i--) {\n    if (src[i] === '}') depth++;\n    else if (src[i] === '{') {\n      if (depth === 0) {\n        start = i;\n        break;\n      }\n      depth--;\n    }\n  }\n  if (start === -1) return '';\n  return balancedGroup(src, start, '{', '}');\n}\n\n/** Every class identifier a file wires as a guard, by any registration form. */\nfunction registrationSites(src: string): Set<string> {\n  const names = new Set<string>();\n  const add = (text: string): void => {\n    for (const id of text.match(IDENT) ?? []) names.add(id);\n  };\n\n  for (const match of src.matchAll(/\\bUseGuards\\s*\\(/g)) {\n    add(balancedGroup(src, (match.index ?? 0) + match[0].length - 1, '(', ')'));\n  }\n  for (const match of src.matchAll(/\\buseGlobalGuards\\s*\\(/g)) {\n    add(balancedGroup(src, (match.index ?? 0) + match[0].length - 1, '(', ')'));\n  }\n  for (const match of src.matchAll(/\\bglobalGuards\\s*:\\s*\\[/g)) {\n    add(balancedGroup(src, (match.index ?? 0) + match[0].length - 1, '[', ']'));\n  }\n  for (const match of src.matchAll(/\\bprovide\\s*:\\s*APP_GUARD\\b/g)) {\n    const provider = enclosingObject(src, match.index ?? 0);\n    for (const bound of provider.matchAll(/\\b(?:useClass|useExisting)\\s*:\\s*([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)/g)) {\n      names.add(bound[1] ?? '');\n    }\n    for (const constructed of provider.matchAll(/\\bnew\\s+([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\s*\\(/g)) {\n      names.add(constructed[1] ?? '');\n    }\n  }\n  return names;\n}\n\ninterface AllowlistEntry {\n  class: string;\n  file: string;\n  owner: string;\n  /** js-yaml parses an unquoted YYYY-MM-DD as a Date; both forms are accepted. */\n  expiry: string | Date;\n  findingId: string;\n  reason: string;\n}\n\nfunction allowlist(): AllowlistEntry[] {\n  const doc = yaml.load(readFileSync(ALLOWLIST, 'utf8')) as { entries?: AllowlistEntry[] };\n  return doc.entries ?? [];\n}\n\nfunction expiryDate(entry: AllowlistEntry): string {\n  return entry.expiry instanceof Date\n    ? entry.expiry.toISOString().slice(0, 10)\n    : String(entry.expiry);\n}\n\nfunction governanceProblems(entry: AllowlistEntry, today: string): string[] {\n  const label = `${entry.class} (${entry.file})`;\n  const problems: string[] = [];\n  for (const key of ['class', 'file', 'owner', 'findingId', 'reason'] as const) {\n    if (typeof entry[key] !== 'string' || entry[key].trim() === '')\n      problems.push(`${label}: missing ${key}`);\n  }\n  const expiry = expiryDate(entry);\n  if (!/^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/.test(expiry)) problems.push(`${label}: expiry is not YYYY-MM-DD`);\n  else if (expiry <= today) problems.push(`${label}: expired on ${expiry}`);\n  return problems;\n}\n\n/** Guards reachable from a registration site, closed over `extends`. */\nfunction reachableGuards(definitions: GuardDefinition[], registered: Set<string>): Set<string> {\n  const byName = new Map(definitions.map((d) => [d.className, d]));\n  const reachable = new Set<string>();\n  const queue = [...registered];\n  while (queue.length > 0) {\n    const name = queue.pop() as string;\n    if (reachable.has(name)) continue;\n    reachable.add(name);\n    const definition = byName.get(name);\n    if (definition?.extendsClass) queue.push(definition.extendsClass);\n  }\n  return reachable;\n}\n\ndescribe('INVARIANT: no dead guards — every CanActivate is wired to a request path or governed (ADR-0010)', () => {\n  const files = listSourceFiles();\n  const sources = new Map(\n    files.map((file) => [file, stripComments(readFileSync(resolve(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8'))]),\n  );\n  const definitions = [...sources.entries()].flatMap(([file, src]) => guardDefinitions(file, src));\n  const registered = new Set<string>();\n  for (const src of sources.values()) {\n    for (const name of registrationSites(src)) registered.add(name);\n  }\n  const reachable = reachableGuards(definitions, registered);\n  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n\n  it('finds the fleet guard inventory (sanity: the scan is not silently empty)', () => {\n    expect(definitions.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);\n    expect(registered.size).toBeGreaterThan(10);\n  });\n\n  it('every allowlist entry is governed (owner, expiry, finding, reason) and names a guard that still exists', () => {\n    const entries = allowlist();\n    const problems = entries.flatMap((entry) => governanceProblems(entry, today));\n    const known = new Set(definitions.map((d) => `${d.className}@${d.file}`));\n    for (const entry of entries) {\n      if (!known.has(`${entry.class}@${entry.file}`)) {\n        problems.push(\n          `${entry.class} (${entry.file}): no such CanActivate class — delete the entry`,\n        );\n      }\n      if (reachable.has(entry.class)) {\n        problems.push(\n          `${entry.class} (${entry.file}): is registered — delete the entry, the guard is live`,\n        );\n      }\n    }\n    expect(problems).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('no CanActivate class in apps/** or libs/** is unregistered and ungoverned', () => {\n    const tolerated = new Set(allowlist().map((entry) => `${entry.class}@${entry.file}`));\n    const dead = definitions\n      .filter((d) => !reachable.has(d.className))\n      .filter((d) => !tolerated.has(`${d.className}@${d.file}`))\n      .map((d) => `${d.className} (${d.file})`)\n      .sort();\n    expect(dead).toEqual([]);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('INVARIANT: both IP access-rule stacks stay deleted (ADR-0010)', () => {\n  /** Identifiers that named the deleted stacks; a list of things that must stay gone. */\n  const RETIRED_IDENTIFIERS: ReadonlyArray<string> = [\n    'IpWhitelistGuard',\n    'BypassIpWhitelist',\n    'IP_WHITELIST_ENABLED',\n    'ip_access_rules',\n    'IpAccessRule',\n    'IpAccessController',\n    'IpAccessService',\n    'settings/ip-access',\n    'IpAccessRulesPage',\n  ];\n  const SEARCH_ROOTS: ReadonlyArray<string> = [\n    'apps',\n    'libs',\n    'platform',\n    'web',\n    'tests',\n    'e2e',\n    'infrastructure',\n    'scripts',\n    'tools',\n    '.claude/allowlists',\n    'docker-compose.droplet.yml',\n    'docker-compose.staging.yml',\n  ];\n  const HISTORICAL_PATH = /(^|\\/)(migrations|\\.archive|dist)\\//;\n\n  function gitGrepFiles(patterns: ReadonlyArray<string>): string[] {\n    try {\n      return execFileSync(\n        'git',\n        [\n          '-C',\n          REPO_ROOT,\n          'grep',\n          '-l',\n          '-F',\n          '--untracked',\n          ...patterns.flatMap((p) => ['-e', p]),\n          '--',\n          ...SEARCH_ROOTS,\n        ],\n        { encoding: 'utf8' },\n      )\n        .split('\\n')\n        .filter(Boolean);\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      const status = (err as { status?: number }).status;\n      if (status === 1) return [];\n      throw err;\n    }\n  }\n\n  it('no live source, test, config or deploy artefact names a retired IP access-rule identifier', () => {\n    const offenders = gitGrepFiles(RETIRED_IDENTIFIERS)\n      .filter((path) => !HISTORICAL_PATH.test(path))\n      .filter((path) => path !== 'tests/invariants/no-dead-guards.spec.ts')\n      // Baseline ledger whose notes narrate past cleanups; history, not code.\n      .filter((path) => path !== 'tools/gates/type-check-spec-baseline.json')\n      // Formatter scope manifest is regenerated from git; stale rows are not code.\n      .filter((path) => path !== 'tools/quality/format-scope.json');\n    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('the retirement migration archives the rules before dropping the table', () => {\n    const migration = readFileSync(\n      resolve(\n        REPO_ROOT,\n        'apps/admin-api-service/src/migrations/1809100000000-RetireIpAccessRules.ts',\n      ),\n      'utf8',\n    );\n    expect(migration).toMatch(\n      /SELECT 'ip_access_rules', to_jsonb\\(t\\) FROM \"admin\"\\.\"ip_access_rules\" t/,\n    );\n    expect(migration).toMatch(/DROP TABLE IF EXISTS \"admin\"\\.\"ip_access_rules\"/);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":333,"startLine":1,"totalLines":333}}}

`apps/${service}/src/app.module.ts`;\n58\t  let src: string;\n59\t  try {\n60\t    src = readFileSync(resolve(REPO_ROOT, path), 'utf8');\n61\t  } catch {\n62\t    return null;\n63\t  }\n64\t  // Drop * Platform-wide invariant — SEC-HIGH-156:\n *\n * Every tenant-scoped subgraph AppModule MUST mount VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware\n * via MiddlewareConsumer.apply(...) in its configure() method, AFTER\n * StripInternalHeadersMiddleware (which sets req.verifiedIdentity) and BEFORE\n * UserContextMiddleware. This makes the gateway-signed x-verified-user-assertion\n * (which binds the user AND the effective tenant into one HMAC-signed blob) the\n * single source of req.user / req.tenantId on every subgraph — instead of the\n * weaker legacy path (raw JWT + a separately-trusted x-tenant-id header).\n *\n * Before this invariant only farm + config mounted the middleware; the other\n * seven tenant-scoped subgraphs leaned on the legacy path, so a compromised\n * intermediary that stripped/forged the tenant header (without the bound\n * assertion) was a larger surface than necessary.\n *\n * # Source-level, same rationale as public-service-edge-hardening.spec.ts:\n * one grep+position pass over every service fires on every PR — fastest\n * feedback for a class of regression that would otherwise ship as a silently\n * weaker trust boundary.\n */\n\nimport { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n\n/**\n * Tenant-scoped subgraphs that receive gateway-federated, tenant-bearing\n * GraphQL and therefore MUST resolve identity from the signed assertion.\n * (gateway-api is the SIGNER; auth-service ISSUES the JWT — neither receives\n * an assertion, so both are intentionally absent.)\n */\nconst SUBGRAPHS_REQUIRED: ReadonlyArray<string> = [\n  'farm-service',\n  'config-service',\n  'billing-service',\n  'sensor-service',\n  'hr-service',\n  'hydroponics-service',\n  'alert-engine',\n  'messaging-service',\n  'ai-service',\n];\n\ninterface ModuleAnalysis {\n  service: string;\n  hasImport: boolean;\n  importsFromCanonical: boolean;\n  hasApplyMount: boolean;\n  /** Strip < VerifiedUserAssertion < UserContext (UserContext optional). */\n  orderedCorrectly: boolean;\n}\n\nfunction analyzeAppModule(service: string): ModuleAnalysis | null {\n  const path = `apps/${service}/src/app.module.ts`;\n  let src: string;\n  try {\n    src = readFileSync(resolve(REPO_ROOT, path), 'utf8');\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n  // Drop block + line comments so docstring mentions of the middleware names do\n  // not register as positional code occurrences.\n  const stripped = src\n    .replace(/\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\//g, '')\n    .split('\\n')\n    .map((line) => line.replace(/(?<!:)\\/\\/.*$/, ''))\n    .join('\\n');\n\n  const M = 'VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware';\n  return {\n    service,\n    hasImport: new RegExp(`import\\\\s+\\\\{[^}]*\\\\b${M}\\\\b[^}]*\\\\}`).test(stripped),\n    importsFromCanonical: new RegExp(\n      `import\\\\s+\\\\{[^}]*\\\\b${M}\\\\b[^}]*\\\\}\\\\s+from\\\\s+['\"]@aquaculture/backend-common/middleware['\"]`,\n    ).test(stripped),\n    hasApplyMount: new RegExp(`\\\\.apply\\\\([\\\\s\\\\S]*?\\\\b${M}\\\\b`).test(stripped),\n    orderedCorrectly: (() => {\n      const configureStart = stripped.indexOf('configure(consumer');\n      if (configureStart === -1) return false;\n      const stripIdx = stripped.indexOf('StripInternalHeadersMiddleware', configureStart);\n      const assertIdx = stripped.indexOf(M, configureStart);\n      if (stripIdx === -1 || assertIdx === -1) return false;\n      // Assertion must run AFTER Strip (it reads req.verifiedIdentity Strip sets).\n      if (assertIdx < stripIdx) return false;\n      // ...and BEFORE UserContext, when the service parses a user payload.\n      const userIdx = stripped.indexOf('UserContextMiddleware', configureStart);\n      if (userIdx === -1) return true;\n      return assertIdx < userIdx;\n    })(),\n  };\n}\n\ndescribe('INVARIANT (SEC-HIGH-156): VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware mounted in every tenant-scoped subgraph', () => {\n  it.each(SUBGRAPHS_REQUIRED)(\n    'subgraph %s mounts VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware after Strip and before UserContext',\n    (service) => {\n      const analysis = analyzeAppModule(service);\n      expect(analysis).not.toBeNull();\n      if (!analysis) return;\n      expect(analysis.hasImport).toBe(true);\n      expect(analysis.importsFromCanonical).toBe(true);\n      expect(analysis.hasApplyMount).toBe(true);\n      expect(analysis.orderedCorrectly).toBe(true);\n    },\n  );\n\n  /**\n   * Exclusion-completeness: a subgraph that ALSO serves a non-gateway public\n   * HTTP surface (a route reached directly by an edge agent / external system,\n   * carrying no gateway service identity) MUST `.exclude()` that surface from\n   * VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware — otherwise it 400s \"requires service\n   * identity\" in production. These are the surfaces a prior revision missed\n   * (sensor /install + /api/devices), so they are pinned here. The ai /api/v2/ai\n   * REST proxy never existed on the gateway and its exclusion was retired.\n   */\n  const REQUIRED_EXCLUSIONS: ReadonlyArray<[string, ReadonlyArray<string>]> = [\n    ['sensor-service', ['mqtt', 'install', 'api/devices']],\n    ['billing-service', ['webhooks']],\n  ];\n\n  it.each(REQUIRED_EXCLUSIONS)(\n    'subgraph %s excludes its non-gateway public routes from the assertion middleware',\n    (service, patterns) => {\n      const src = readFileSync(\n        resolve(REPO_ROOT, `apps/${service}/src/app.module.ts`),\n        'utf8',\n      );\n      // The exclude must scope the assertion middleware specifically (a 3-way\n      // split), so the file carries a `.exclude(` listing each public prefix.\n      for (const pattern of patterns) {\n        expect(src).toMatch(new RegExp(`\\\\.exclude\\\\([^)]*['\"]${pattern}['\"]`, 's'));\n      }\n    },\n  );\n\n  it('the canonical middleware lives at libs/backend-common/src/middleware', () => {\n    const lsFiles = execFileSync(\n      'git',\n      [\n        '-C',\n        REPO_ROOT,\n        'ls-files',\n        'libs/backend-common/src/middleware/verified-user-assertion.middleware.ts',\n      ],\n      { encoding: 'utf8' },\n    ).trim();\n    expect(lsFiles).toBe(\n      'libs/backend-common/src/middleware/verified-user-assertion.middleware.ts',\n    );\n  });\n});\n","numLines":155,"startLine":1,"totalLines":155}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PwfFkAfSmVx2MbB1JtxBCW","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * HRResolver — employee contact-PII object-level authz (DB-PEOPLE-MEDIUM-001).\n3\t *\n4\t * The employee read queries are gated to the broad MODULE_USER role. Before this\n5\t * fix, any MODULE_USER could read every colleague's home address, personal\n6\t * phone, and emergency contacts. These tests pin that full contact PII reaches\n7\t * only workforce managers (TENANT_ADMIN/MODULE_MANAGER) or the subject\n8\t * themselves; everyone else gets a redacted directory projection.\n9\t */\n10\timport { HRResolver } from '../hr.resolver';\n11\timport { Role } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/decorators';\n12\timport type { CurrentUserPayload } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/decorators';\n13\timport { Employee } from '../entities/employee.entity';\n14\t\n15\tconst context = { req: { user: { sub: 'viewer-user', tenantId: 't1' } } } as never;\n16\t\n17\tfunction makeEmployee(over: Partial<Employee> = {}): Employee {\n18\t  return {\n19\t    id: 'emp-1',\n20\t    tenantId: 't1',\n21\t    userId: 'other-user',\n22\t    firstName: 'Ada',\n23\t    lastName: 'Lovelace',\n24\t    email: 'ada@farm.test',\n25\t    contactInfo: {\n26\t      email: 'ada@farm.test',\n27\t      phone: '+1-555-0100',\n28\t      emergencyContact: 'Charles',\n29\t      emergencyPhone: '+1-555-0199',\n30\t    },\n31\t    address: {\n32\t      street: '12 Analytical Ave',\n33\t      city: 'London',\n34\t      state: 'LDN',\n35\t      postalCode: 'EC1',\n36\t      country: 'UK',\n37\t    },\n38\t    ...over,\n39\t  } as Employee;\n40\t}\n41\t\n42\tfunction makeViewer(roles: string[], sub = 'viewer-user'): CurrentUserPayload {\n43\t  return { sub, email: 'v@farm.test', tenantId: 't1', roles } as CurrentUserPayload;\n44\t}\n45\t\n46\tfunction makeResolver(employee: Employee) {\n47\t  const queryBus = { execute: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(employee) };\n48\t  const commandBus = { execute: jest.fn() };\n49\t  return new HRResolver(commandBus as never, queryBus as never);\n50\t}\n51\t\n52\tdescribe('HRResolver employee contact-PII masking', () => {\n53\t  it('TENANT_ADMIN sees full contact PII', async () => {\n54\t    const emp = makeEmployee();\n55\t    const resolver = makeResolver(emp);\n56\t    const result = await resolver.getEmployee('emp-1', context, makeViewer([Role.TENANT_ADMIN]));\n57\t    expect(result.contactInfo.phone).toBe('+1-555-0100');\n58\t    expect(result.address.street).toBe('12 Analytical Ave');\n59\t  });\n60\t\n61\t  it('MODULE_MANAGER sees full contact PII', async () => {\n62\t    const emp = makeEmployee();\n63\t    const resolver = makeResolver(emp);\n64\t    const result = await resolver.getEmployee('emp-1', context, makeViewer([Role.MODULE_MANAGER]));\n65\t    expect(result.contactInfo.phone).toBe('+1-555-0100');\n66\t    expect(result.address.city).toBe('London');\n67\t  });\n68\t\n69\t  it('MODULE_USER viewing their OWN record sees full contact PII', async () => {\n70\t    const emp = makeEmployee({ userId: expect(result.contactInfo.phone).toBe('+1-555-0100');\n74\t    expect(result.address.street).toBe('12 Analytical Ave');\n75\t  });\n76\t\n77\t  it('MODULE_USER viewing ANOTHER employee gets a redacted resolver.getEmployee('emp-1', context, makeViewer([Role.MODULE_USER]));\n81\t    // Work email stays (already public via the top-level email field)...\n82\t    expect(result.contactInfo.email).toBe('ada@farm.test');\n83\t    // ...but personal + emergency phone and the full home address are redacted.\n84\t    expect(result.contactInfo.phone).toBe('REDACTED');\n85\t    expect(result.contactInfo.emergencyContact).toBeUndefined();\n86\t    expect(result.contactInfo.emergencyPhone).toBeUndefined();\n87\t * HRResolver — employee contact-PII object-level authz (DB-PEOPLE-MEDIUM-001).\n *\n * The employee read queries are gated to the broad MODULE_USER role. Before this\n * fix, any MODULE_USER could read every colleague's home address, personal\n * phone, and emergency contacts. These tests pin that full contact PII reaches\n * only workforce managers (TENANT_ADMIN/MODULE_MANAGER) or the subject\n * themselves; everyone else gets a redacted directory projection.\n */\nimport { HRResolver } from '../hr.resolver';\nimport { Role } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/decorators';\nimport type { CurrentUserPayload } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/decorators';\nimport { Employee } from '../entities/employee.entity';\n\nconst context = { req: { user: { sub: 'viewer-user', tenantId: 't1' } } } as never;\n\nfunction makeEmployee(over: Partial<Employee> = {}): Employee {\n  return {\n    id: 'emp-1',\n    tenantId: 't1',\n    userId: 'other-user',\n    firstName: 'Ada',\n    lastName: 'Lovelace',\n    email: 'ada@farm.test',\n    contactInfo: {\n      email: 'ada@farm.test',\n      phone: '+1-555-0100',\n      emergencyContact: 'Charles',\n      emergencyPhone: '+1-555-0199',\n    },\n    address: {\n      street: '12 Analytical Ave',\n      city: 'London',\n      state: 'LDN',\n      postalCode: 'EC1',\n      country: 'UK',\n    },\n    ...over,\n  } as Employee;\n}\n\nfunction makeViewer(roles: string[], sub = 'viewer-user'): CurrentUserPayload {\n  return { sub, email: 'v@farm.test', tenantId: 't1', roles } as CurrentUserPayload;\n}\n\nfunction makeResolver(employee: Employee) {\n  const queryBus = { execute: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(employee) };\n  const commandBus = { execute: jest.fn() };\n  return new HRResolver(commandBus as never, queryBus as never);\n}\n\ndescribe('HRResolver employee contact-PII masking', () => {\n  it('TENANT_ADMIN sees full contact PII', async () => {\n    const emp = makeEmployee();\n    const resolver = makeResolver(emp);\n    const result = await resolver.getEmployee('emp-1', context, makeViewer([Role.TENANT_ADMIN]));\n    expect(result.contactInfo.phone).toBe('+1-555-0100');\n    expect(result.address.street).toBe('12 Analytical Ave');\n  });\n\n  it('MODULE_MANAGER sees full contact PII', async () => {\n    const emp = makeEmployee();\n    const resolver = makeResolver(emp);\n    const result = await resolver.getEmployee('emp-1', context, makeViewer([Role.MODULE_MANAGER]));\n    expect(result.contactInfo.phone).toBe('+1-555-0100');\n    expect(result.address.city).toBe('London');\n  });\n\n  it('MODULE_USER viewing their OWN record sees full contact PII', async () => {\n    const emp = makeEmployee({ userId: 'viewer-user' });\n    const resolver = makeResolver(emp);\n    const result = await resolver.getEmployee('emp-1', context, makeViewer([Role.MODULE_USER]));\n    expect(result.contactInfo.phone).toBe('+1-555-0100');\n    expect(result.address.street).toBe('12 Analytical Ave');\n  });\n\n  it('MODULE_USER viewing ANOTHER employee gets a redacted directory projection', async () => {\n    const emp = makeEmployee({ userId: 'other-user' });\n    const resolver = makeResolver(emp);\n    const result = await resolver.getEmployee('emp-1', context, makeViewer([Role.MODULE_USER]));\n    // Work email stays (already public via the top-level email field)...\n    expect(result.contactInfo.email).toBe('ada@farm.test');\n    // ...but personal + emergency phone and the full home address are redacted.\n    expect(result.contactInfo.phone).toBe('REDACTED');\n    expect(result.contactInfo.emergencyContact).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(result.contactInfo.emergencyPhone).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(result.address.street).toBe('REDACTED');\n    expect(result.address.postalCode).toBe('REDACTED');\n    expect(result.address.city).toBe('REDACTED');\n  });","numLines":90,"startLine":1,"totalLines":109}}}
